Result: 仅用单一小缺陷超胞训练的MLIP处理大超胞时误差严重；加入多个超胞尺寸的缺陷数据可改善预测性能，再补充无缺陷体相超胞数据可进一步提升精度。最终方案仅需四次DFT弛豫即可训练出在超过200原子大超胞中缺陷形成能误差大多小于0.3 eV的MLIP。

Conclusion: 该工作为以较低计算成本训练缺陷体系MLIP模型提供了实用指导，展示了小超胞+有限DFT数据训练方案在中性和低电荷态点缺陷模拟中的有效性与外推能力。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) can treat large-scale material systems with near first-principles accuracy and have been widely used to accelerate point-defect simulations. However, the training of MLIPs usually relies on large amounts of DFT data. This issue is particularly pronounced for charged defects, for which DFT calculations of large supercells are required to avoid long-range Coulomb interactions and finite-size effects, making the construction of datasets computationally expensive. In this work, we propose an efficient MLIP training scheme for neutral and lowly charged point defects based on small supercells (less than 100 atoms) and limited number of DFT calculations. The scheme requires only four DFT structural relaxations to construct the training dataset and the trained MLIPs dedicated for the defect can predict the defect formation energies in larger supercells (over 200 atoms) with small errors (mostly smaller than 0.3 eV). Using defects in GaN, SiO$_2$, and $\mathrm{Cu}_2\mathrm{ZnSnS}_4$ as representative examples, we evaluate the extrapolation capability of this scheme for predicting defect total energies and structural relaxations across different supercell sizes. The results show that an MLIP trained only on single small defect supercell produces severe errors when treating large supercells. Incorporating defect data from multiple supercell sizes improves the predictive performance of the MLIP, while adding pristine defect-free bulk supercells further enhances the accuracy. These results provide practical guidance for training MLIP models of defect systems with low computational costs.

</details>


### [90] [Vacancy aggregation enhances NV- spin coherence in diamond: a cluster-correlation-expansion study of multi-vacancy spin baths in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24295)
*Chikara Shinei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过对多空位复合体自旋浴的簇关联展开模拟，作者推翻了"空位聚集导致NV-退相干"的传统观点，发现多空位浴反而使Hahn回波相干时间比孤立负空位浴延长2.7–4.4倍，且该比值在三个数量级浓度范围内保持恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究普遍将辐照/注入金刚石中NV-的退相干归咎于可移动空位聚集形成的多空位自旋中心，并有两项相干性纪录被归功于去除这些中心。作者质疑这一归因，希望定量检验多空位复合体与孤立负空位对自旋量子比特退相干的影响差异。

Method: 采用簇关联展开（cluster-correlation-expansion）模拟，并利用已发表的电子顺磁共振（EPR）参数驱动计算。在固定顺磁自旋密度下，比较两种自旋浴对NV-中心Hahn回波相干时间的影响：孤立负空位浴与多空位复合体浴；并区分同一晶格位点与对称等价位点两种情形，分析自旋投影交换、跃迁频率匹配及精细结构劈裂的贡献。

Result: 在固定顺磁自旋密度下，多空位浴的Hahn回波相干时间比孤立负空位浴长2.7–4.4倍，且该比值在三个数量级浓度范围内保持恒定。退相干由浴内自旋间的自旋投影交换引起，而交换仅在跃迁频率匹配时发生。精细结构劈裂带来的频率移动对相干时间的贡献仅为1.3倍；真实缺陷占据不同取向的对称等价位点，导致邻近自旋频率失配而停止交换，额外贡献1.9倍。因此相干时间由共享跃迁频率的浴内自旋对所占比例决定，而非零场劈裂。

Conclusion: 空位聚集并非缩短而是延长了NV-的自旋相干时间；决定相干时间的关键是浴内跃迁频率匹配的自旋对比例，而非零场劈裂。这一结论推翻了此前将NV-退相干归因于多空位中心的观点，并有望指导通过刻意调控空位聚集来进一步提升金刚石自旋量子比特的相干性能。

Abstract: Annealing a semiconductor makes its vacancies mobile; they aggregate into multi-vacancy complexes that often carry spin. Such centres are a magnetic-noise source for any spin qubit among them, in silicon and silicon carbide as well as in diamond, and they are accordingly blamed for NV- decoherence in irradiated and implanted diamond, with two coherence records credited to removing them. Cluster-correlation-expansion simulations driven by published electron-paramagnetic-resonance parameters invert that attribution. At a fixed paramagnetic spin density a multi-vacancy bath gives a Hahn-echo coherence time 2.7-4.4 times longer than a bath of isolated negative vacancies, constant over three decades of concentration. A bath dephases the qubit because its spins exchange spin projections with one another, and two can exchange only if their transition frequencies match. A fine-structure splitting shifts those frequencies. Were every defect on the same crystallographic site, all would shift alike and still match: worth only a factor 1.3. Real defects occupy symmetry-equivalent sites pointing in different directions, so neighbours land at different frequencies and stop exchanging: a further 1.9. What governs the coherence time is therefore the fraction of bath pairs sharing a transition frequency, not the zero-field splitting. Vacancy aggregation extends NV- spin coherence rather than shortening it.

</details>


### [91] [Axial thermal expansion from free-energy minimization with temperature-dependent force constants in phonopy: a technical report, with $α$- and $ω$-Ti as the worked example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24336)
*Atsushi Togo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍在 phonopy 中实现的一种方法：通过最小化亥姆霍兹自由能来求六方晶体随温度变化的平衡晶格常数 a 和 c，并由此计算轴向热膨胀系数。以钛的 α 相和 ω 相为例，分析热膨胀行为及计算中的数值敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方晶体的 a 轴与 c 轴热膨胀速率不同，需用两个独立的轴向热膨胀系数描述，而这两个轴在自由能中相互耦合，使轴向系数难以准确计算。现有方法（纯谐波力常数与自洽谐波近似）的差异及其对系数的影响有待系统评估。

Method: 在有限组 (a, c) 取值上计算谐波力常数得到自由能，并在 (a, c) 空间拟合自由能曲面，通过最小化自由能得到各温度下的平衡晶格参数；轴向系数取晶格参数对温度的导数。变体中，谐波力常数由自洽谐波近似得到的温度相关力常数替代，力则由在每个晶格参数下拟合的机器学习势计算。以钛的 α 相和 ω 相为算例。

Result: 两种计算均显示 α-Ti 的 c 轴在低温下呈负热膨胀，而 ω-Ti 无此现象；两方法差异在 α-Ti 的 c 轴最大。轴向系数的计算比体积系数更难，因为在两种钛相中使 a 轴伸长的热效应会同时使 c 轴缩短，c 轴系数为两个符号相反、部分抵消的项之和，任一项的小误差都会导致系数较大的相对误差。报告量化了程序各设置对热膨胀系数的影响，其中 α-Ti 的 c 轴最敏感。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效平衡六方晶体的两轴自由能并计算轴向热膨胀系数，但 c 轴系数因正负项相消而对误差极其敏感，是计算中最需谨慎处理的量。

Abstract: In a hexagonal crystal the $a$ and $c$ axes change their lengths at different rates with increasing temperature, and the thermal expansion is described by two coefficients, one for each axis. The equilibrium lattice parameters at each temperature are found by minimizing the Helmholtz free energy over the lattice parameters, and the coefficients are their logarithmic derivatives with respect to temperature. This report describes a procedure for this minimization as implemented in phonopy. The free energy is computed with harmonic force constants at a finite set of values of $a$ and $c$, and a free-energy surface over $a$ and $c$ is fitted to these values. In a variant, the harmonic force constants are replaced by temperature-dependent force constants from a self-consistent harmonic approximation, with the forces computed by a machine-learning potential fitted at each of these values. The $α$ and $ω$ phases of titanium are the worked example. Both calculations give negative thermal expansion of the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti at low temperature and none in $ω$-Ti, and they differ most for the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti. The axial coefficients are harder to compute than the volumetric one, because the two axes are coupled in the free energy. In both phases of titanium the thermal effect that lengthens $a$ also shortens $c$. The coefficient of the $c$ axis is then the sum of two terms of opposite sign that partly cancel, and a small error in either term gives a large relative error in the coefficient. The report measures how much each setting of the procedure changes the thermal expansion coefficients, with the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti as the most sensitive case.

</details>


### [92] [On the dual character of Zn impurity in SnTe: Tuning thermoelectric and topological properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24459)
*Kacper Pryga,Bartlomiej Wiendlocha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SnTe 是无铅的 PbTe 类似物，是有前景的热电材料。实验报道 Zn 掺杂能提升 p 型样品的性能，但其微观机理尚不清晰，需要从第一性原理层面阐明 Zn 在 SnTe 中的作用，并探索掺杂对热电与拓扑性质的协同调控机制。

Method: 采用 Korringa-Kohn-Rostoker（KKR）方法结合相干势近似（CPA）进行第一性原理计算，并用赝势计算加以补充，系统研究 Zn 掺杂浓度对 SnTe 电子结构、能带拓扑序和热电输运性质（Seebeck 系数、双极效应）的影响。

Result: 1. Zn 在导带内引入类共振杂质态，且随掺杂浓度强烈演化，显著增强 n 型 SnTe 的热电势；2. 价带中 Zn 掺杂诱导 L-Σ 能带汇聚，在宽浓度范围内提升 p 型 Seebeck 系数并推迟双极效应的出现；3. Zn 诱导的能带结构改变驱动能带从反转序转变为平庸序，表明存在可调控的拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了 Zn 在 SnTe 中的微观作用，证实掺杂可同时调控热电性能与拓扑性质，为通过 Zn 掺杂优化 SnTe 基热电材料并控制其拓扑相提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a first-principles study of the electronic structure and thermoelectric properties of Zn-doped SnTe using the Korringa-Kohn-Rostoker method within the coherent potential approximation, complemented by pseudopotential calculations. SnTe is a lead-free analogue of PbTe and a candidate thermoelectric material in which Zn doping has been experimentally reported to enhance the performance of $p$-type samples. We show that Zn introduces a resonant-like impurity state, located within the conduction band, which evolves strongly depending on the Zn concentration. This feature leads to a significant enhancement of the thermopower in $n$-type SnTe. In the valence band, Zn doping induces L-$Σ$ band convergence, also resulting in an increased $p$-type Seebeck coefficient over a broad concentration range and delaying the onset of the bipolar effect. We further demonstrate that the Zn-induced band-structure modifications drive a transition from an inverted to a trivial band ordering, indicating a controllable topological phase transition. Our results clarify the microscopic role of Zn in SnTe and identify doping as a mechanism for simultaneously tuning thermoelectric and topological properties.

</details>


### [93] [III-V antiphase boundaries are not generated by Si or Ge substrate step edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24792)
*Charles Cornet,Sreejith Pallikkara Chandrasekharan,Audrey Gilbert,Milan Silvestre,Rozenn Bernard,Pascal Turban,Gilles Patriarche,Eric Tournie,Laurent Pedesseau,Jean-Baptiste Rodriguez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>

Motivation: III-V族半导体与IV族衬底之间存在价态和晶格结构不匹配，导致异质界面处易形成反相畴界（APBs）等缺陷。传统观点认为反相畴界在单原子台阶边缘生成，但这一机制与最新实验观测存在矛盾，亟需重新审视并建立统一的生长理论框架，以指导III-V/IV异质集成的实现。

Result: 分析表明，异质界面的形成由衬底台面的重构所主导，反相畴界并非在单原子台阶边缘产生，推翻了以往关于反相畴界起源的认识；提出了一个广义化的III-V/IV生长框架。

Conclusion: 该广义化的III-V/IV生长框架为III-V族半导体在IV族平台上的集成提供了新见解，并对更广泛的价态不匹配异质外延具有普遍指导意义。

Abstract: We critically review recent experimental and theoretical advances on the valence- and structure-mismatched heteroepitaxy of III-V semiconductors on group-IV substrates. We examine key aspects including wetting behavior, atomic configurations at the hetero-interface, substrate passivation, and the stability of antiphase boundaries (APBs). By synthesizing these findings with pioneering studies, we propose a refined description of antiphase boundaries formation. Our analysis shows that hetero-interface formation is dictated by substrate terrace reconstruction, demonstrating that APBs are not generated at monoatomic step edges. This generalized III-V/IV growth framework provides new insights for the integration of III-V semiconductors on group-IV platforms and, more broadly, for valence-mismatched heteroepitaxy.

Abstract: We report the properties of hexagonal (space group P6/mmm) scandium diboride ($\mathrm{ScB}_2$) single crystals grown by a laser diode floating zone method at growth rates of ~1mm/hr under B-rich conditions with (002) rocking curve widths $Δω$=38'' approaching the quality of commercial SiC/GaN substrates. Lattice expansion measurements reveal matching to $\mathrm{Al_{0.55}Ga_{0.45}N}$ with a coefficient of thermal expansion ~5ppm/K at typical AlGaN growth temperatures, enabling thick AlGaN layers for ultra-wide bandgap (UWBG) power electronics >1kV. We measure semi-metallic room temperature resistivity ~15$μΩ$ cm, climbing to ~93$μΩ$ cm at 773K with a $T^2$ dependence effectively eliminating substrate parasitic resistance, the limiting factor in exploiting the full potential of UWBG. The Debye temperature $θ_{D,ScB_2}$ from heat capacity and lattice expansion is ~850K well matched to $θ_{D,ScB_2}$, but lower than the 1100K measured for Sc-rich growth conditions. We discuss Debye matching as a key substrate codesign criterion providing significant overlap in phonon modes for heat removal and thermal matching during AlGaN growth. The competitive thermal conductivity at room temperature 53W/mK is half that from full first principles calculations, a discrepancy we attribute to the presence of Sc-vacancies generated by B-rich growth. while the resistivity is ~2x the theoretical value, indicating that both electrons and phonons play equal role in thermal transport. The smooth ~2.5nm rms roughness surface enables advanced heat removal modalities through engineered phonon bridges and phonon polaritons in $\mathrm{ScB}_2$/AlGaN interfacial heterostructures, potentially allowing ~10-100x increase in power handling over state-of-the-art GaN/SiC.

Abstract: Tantalum carbide (TaC) thin film ''virtual'' substrates are a highly desirable material for $\text{Al}_{0.5}\text{Ga}_{0.5}\text{N}$ vertical power electronics devices due to lattice matching, thermal expansion matching, and metallic conductivity. However, the material has not been demonstrated to support $\text{Al}_{x}\text{Ga}_{1-x}\text{N}$ layers of variable composition $x$, limiting the range of device applications. We present a method to achieve tunable rock salt $\text{Ta}\text{C}_{x}\text{N}_{1-x}$ virtual substrates via a face-to-face annealing of TaC thin films in an $\text{N}_2$ atmosphere. The results suggest that annealing temperatures below $1600\,^{\circ}\text{C}$ promote partial uptake of nitrogen onto carbon and anion vacancy sites to form rock salt $\text{Ta(C,N)}$ with intermediate anion compositions. At temperatures $\ge 1600\,^{\circ}\text{C}$, nitrogen primarily occupies the anion sublattice and the crystalline quality and surface morphology simultaneously degrade coincident with the formation of secondary phases. This study demonstrates the growth and processing parameters necessary to make tunable lattice constant virtual substrates for $\text{Al}_{x}\text{Ga}_{1-x}\text{N}$ from $x = 0.5\text{-}1$, enabling vertically conducting power electronic devices with reduced defect density at high Al-content.

### [96] [Universal Response Functions in Driven Dissipative Tunneling Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22328)
*Krishna Kingkar Pathak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明周期性驱动与欧姆耗散耦合下，势垒穿越过程由显式的双参数普适响应函数支配，而非仅是修正的指数标度；隧穿指数可因式分解为系统依赖的静态项与两个无量纲控制参数（归一化驱动频率与耗散强度）的普适函数，构成一个独特的双参数普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性非平衡系统的普适性通常由标度律体现，但当周期性驱动与非局域耗散记忆同时作用时，这一普适性是否仍然成立，是有驱动的开放动力学中尚未解决的核心问题。

Method: 在融合Floquet调制与欧姆环境耦合的半经典瞬子（instanton）框架下展开研究：隧穿指数被分解为系统依赖的静态贡献与一个只依赖归一化驱动频率和耗散强度两个无量纲参数的普适函数；通过单一鞍点轨迹的联合修正实现因式分解，并以弱到中等耗散作为有效作用的平滑动力学重整化，保留鞍点结构并支持受控解析展开；高频区呈现普适动力学平均，显式积分表示给出从绝热到Floquet的连续过渡；再通过对非局域瞬子作用的直接数值计算加以验证。

Result: 隧穿指数精确因式分解，未引入额外独立标度变量；弱至中等耗散保持鞍点结构并使解析展开可行；高频极限下响应呈现普适动力学平均，并存在连续的绝热—Floquet过渡；直接数值计算确认归一化隧穿指数在不同模型系统间对驱动参数呈普适依赖。

Conclusion: 有驱动的耗散势垒穿越被识别为非线性非平衡动力学中的一个独特双参数普适类，并为具有记忆的驱动系统响应现象提供了预测性的函数框架。

Abstract: Universality in nonlinear nonequilibrium systems is typically expressed through scaling laws that render macroscopic behavior insensitive to microscopic details. Whether this universality survives when periodic forcing and nonlocal dissipative memory act simultaneously remains an open question in driven open dynamics. Here, we demonstrate that barrier-crossing processes in periodically driven dissipative systems are governed not merely by modified exponential scaling, but by an explicit two-parameter universal response function.
  Within a semiclassical instanton framework incorporating Floquet modulation and Ohmic environmental coupling, the tunneling exponent factorizes into a system-dependent static contribution and a universal function of two dimensionless control parameters: normalized driving frequency and dissipation strength. This factorization arises from the combined modification of a single saddle-point trajectory and introduces no additional independent scaling variables.
  Weak-to-moderate dissipation acts as a smooth dynamical renormalization of the effective action, preserving the saddle-point structure and enabling controlled analytical expansion. In the high-frequency regime, the response exhibits universal dynamical averaging, while an explicit integral representation establishes a continuous adiabatic to Floquet crossover. Direct numerical evaluation of the nonlocal instanton action confirms that the normalized tunneling exponent exhibits a universal dependence on the driving parameters across different model systems. These results identify driven dissipative barrier crossing as a distinct two-parameter universality class within nonlinear nonequilibrium dynamics and provide a predictive functional framework for response phenomena in driven systems with memory.

</details>


### [97] [Diffusion with stochastic resetting in the presence of a delta killing trap: Drift-controlled survival regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22527)
*Alain Mazzolo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维扩散粒子在随机重置与局部不完全吸收靶（δ函数杀死率）下的存活行为，推导任意参数下的 Laplace 空间精确解，并揭示存活概率在扩散复发、漂移瞬态与重置更新三种机制间的统一交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机重置如何与漂移引起的瞬态性、靶的有限反应性（不完全吸收）相竞争，从而决定粒子最终的存活概率与长期衰减规律。

Method: 对受 δ 函数杀死率建模的不完全局部靶，结合随机重置与恒定漂移，推导非归一化传播子与存活概率的 Laplace 空间精确表达式；通过分析 Laplace 变换的最右极点确定指数衰减率 θ，并给出归一化后的准稳态密度分布，涵盖任意扩散系数 D、重置率 r 与杀死强度 k。

Result: 无重置时，无偏扩散呈循环性代数衰减 S(t)∼t^{-1/2}，而任意非零漂移导致瞬态并保留非零最终存活概率；相反，任意 r>0 且 k>0 通过反复更新相遇恢复最终吸收，给出指数衰减 S(t)∼A e^{-θt}。无漂移与完全吸收极限恢复标准重置结果。

Conclusion: 所得到的公式统一描述了由循环性、瞬态性与更新机制控制的存活行为之间的交叉，为随机重置下不完全靶吸收问题提供了完整解析刻画。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional diffusive particle subject to stochastic resetting to its initial position, in the presence of an imperfect, localized target that can absorb (kill) the particle, modeled by a delta-function killing rate. The central question is how stochastic resetting competes with drift-induced transience and the target's finite reactivity. Exact Laplace-space expressions are derived for the non-normalized propagator and survival probability, for arbitrary diffusion coefficient $D$, resetting rate $r$, and killing strength $k$, first in the absence of drift and then under a constant drift. The long-time behavior separates into distinct regimes. Without resetting, unbiased diffusion exhibits the recurrent algebraic survival law $S(t)\sim t^{-1/2}$, whereas any nonzero drift renders the motion transient with respect to the target and leaves a nonzero ultimate survival probability. By contrast, any $r>0$ and $k>0$ repeatedly renews encounters with the target and restores eventual absorption, yielding an exponential survival law $S(t)\sim A e^{-θt}$. The decay rate $θ$ is given by the dominant (rightmost) pole of the Laplace transform, and the surviving density converges after normalization to an explicit quasi-stationary profile. The driftless and perfectly absorbing limits recover standard resetting results. These formulas provide a unified description of the crossover between recurrence-controlled, transience-controlled, and renewal-controlled survival.

</details>


### [98] [Intermittency in Wind-Driven Fires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22541)
*Laurent Hébert-Dufresne,Aanjaneya Kumar,S. Redner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一维风驱动森林火灾模型，研究火势可跨越间隙点燃下风处森林时的动力学行为，发现随闪电频率变化系统呈现三种不同相态：准确定性动力学、间歇性与混沌稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实森林火灾中，风可使火势跨越空旷间隙点燃下风处的树木，这一跨间隙点火机制常常被传统森林火灾模型忽略。作者旨在构建能刻画该机制的模型，揭示其导致的非平凡动力学相变与统计行为。

Method: 构建一维风驱动森林火灾模型：火可跳过树木之间的间隙点燃不相连的下风森林。可跳过的间隙大小取决于火强度——火势在树木中传播时强度增大，跳过间隙时强度减弱。树木在空位上以速率r生长，闪电以速率f雷击每个位点。

Result: 当f≪r/L（L为系统长度）时，闪电足够罕见，出现准确定性动力学，每次雷击引发的火灾会烧毁所有树木；当f≫L^{-μ}（μ≈0.8）时，闪电足够频繁，系统达到稳态，但森林尺寸分布与间隙尺寸分布表现出意外行为；两者之间存在间歇性，共存确定性与混沌动力学的时序畴（temporal domains）。

Conclusion: 风驱动的跨间隙点火机制使一维森林火灾模型呈现丰富的三相行为：准确定性、间歇性及具有反常尺寸分布的稳态。闪电频率是控制相变的关键参数，间歇态中确定性与混沌时序畴共存。

Abstract: We construct a wind-driven forest-fire model in one dimension in which a fire can jump gaps between trees to ignite disjoint downwind forests. The size of a gap that a fire can jump depends on the fire intensity, which increases as the fire propagates through trees and diminishes as the fire jumps gaps. Trees grow on empty sites at rate $r$ and lightning strikes each site with rate $f$. When $f\ll r/L$, where $L$ is the system length, lightning is sufficiently rare that quasi-deterministic dynamics arises where all trees are consumed when a lightning-induced fire occurs. For $f\gg L^{-μ}$ with $μ\approx 0.8$, lightning is sufficiently frequent that a steady state is reached, but with unexpected behaviors for the forest- and gap-size distributions. Intermittency arises in between these regimes, with coexisting temporal domains of deterministic and chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Long-range self-avoiding walk in one dimension: a Monte Carlo study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23346)
*Jiang Zhou,Ziyu Liu,Pengcheng Hou,Zhijie Fan,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟研究一维长程自回避行走（SAW），跳跃长度按 r^{-(d+σ)} 代数衰减。结果显示 σ>1 时保持短程普适类，σ≤1 时偏离短程行为；在 1/2<σ≤1 的长程 Wilson-Fisher 区域，反常维度 η 符合长程高斯不动点预测 η=2-σ，而 Binder 比值和 ν 随 σ 非平凡变化并在 σ=1 处出现不连续跳变，与长程 O(n) 模型的 (d,σ) 普适性图景及 n→0 极限一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清一维长程自回避行走（即长程 O(n) 模型在 n→0 极限下的情况）中，长程与短程普适类的分界位置以及各临界量的 σ 依赖行为，从而验证最近提出的 (d,σ) 平面普适性图景。

Method: 采用巨正则系综下的一维长程自回避行走模型（跳跃长度 r 的统计权重按 r^{-(d+σ)} 衰减），利用带高效不可逆更新方案的大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，高精度估计临界逸度 z_c、普适 Binder 比值 Q_N^c、关联长度指数 ν 和反常维度 η。

Result: σ>1 时 z_c 随 σ 平滑变化，Binder 比值与临界指数保持短程普适类；σ≤1 时结果明显偏离短程行为，确认 σ=1 为长程与短程区域的分界。在 1/2<σ≤1 的长程 Wilson-Fisher 区域，η 符合长程高斯不动点预测 η_GFP=2-σ，而 Q_N^c 与 ν 随 σ 非平凡变化并在 σ=1 处有不连续跳变。

Conclusion: 研究发现与最近提出的长程 O(n) 模型 (d,σ) 平面普适性图景高度吻合，自回避行走对应 n→0 极限；σ=1 是长程与短程普适类的分界，且长程 Wilson-Fisher 区域内 η 由高斯不动点决定，而 ν 和 Binder 比值显示出非平凡的 σ 依赖与 σ=1 处的不连续性。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional long-range self-avoiding walk in the grand-canonical ensemble where the statistical weight of a jump of length $r$ decays algebraically as $r^{-(d+σ)}$. Using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations with an efficient irreversible update scheme, we obtain high-precision estimates of the critical fugacity $z_c$, the universal Binder ratio $Q_N^c$, the correlation-length exponent $ν$, and the anomalous dimension $η$. For $σ>1$, the critical fugacity varies smoothly with $σ$, while the Binder ratio and the critical exponents remain consistent with the short-range universality class. For $σ\le 1$, by contrast, the results clearly depart from short-range behavior, identifying $σ=1$ as the boundary between long-range and short-range regimes. In the long-range Wilson-Fisher regime with $1/2 < σ\leq 1$, $η$ agrees with the long-range Gaussian-fixed-point prediction $η_{\mathrm{GFP}}=2-σ$, whereas $Q_N^c$ and $ν$ vary nontrivially with $σ$ and exhibit discontinuous jumps at $σ=1$. These findings are in good agreement with the recently proposed universality diagram in the $(d,σ)$ plane for long-range O$(n)$ models, with the self-avoiding walk corresponding to the $n\to0$ limit.

</details>


### [100] [Critical Touching of Temporal Entanglement Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23513)
*Ting-Long Wang,Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuai Yin,Yi-Fan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究周期性驱动横场J1-J2 Ising链中的时间纠缠相变（TET），发现两个一阶型TET可以合并为一种“临界接触”（critical touching）——即领先Schmidt能级相切而不交叉，实现连续相变的时域类比。临界频率附近两TET的时间间隔连续趋于零且其对频率的导数发散，呈现单一阶TET所没有的非解析特征。通过二阶Floquet哈密顿量可理解该现象，并存在一个无TET的有限频率窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有时间纠缠相变（TET）均表现为不同对称扇区领先Schmidt能级的线性交叉，类似平衡态中的一阶相变。作者提出并探讨：是否存在以纠缠谱“接触”（touching）为特征的连续型TET，即领先能级相切而不交换的时域连续相变。

Method: 在周期性驱动的横场J1-J2伊辛链中，通过扫描驱动频率，考察时间纠缠谱中领先Schmidt能级的行为；利用对称性保持的微扰对乘积初态的作用与Floquet修正的相互作用，并由二阶Floquet哈密顿量进行解析理解。

Result: 发现两个一阶型TET可合并为临界接触（critical touching），领先能级相切而不交换；临界频率附近两TET的时间间隔连续消失，且其对频率的导数发散，呈现非解析特征。进一步扩展频率扫描，在更高频率处出现第二个临界接触，两个临界频率之间形成一个无TET的有限窗口。这些特征在不同耦合比下广泛存在。

Conclusion: 临界接触是时间纠缠相变的一种独特形式，实现了平衡态连续相变的时域类比。其非解析特征（时间间隔导数发散）区别于单一阶TET，且现象可被二阶Floquet哈密顿量解释，并在宽泛耦合比范围内稳定存在，确立了时间纠缠相变的新分类。

Abstract: Equilibrium phase transitions are conventionally categorized into first-order and continuous phase transitions. Far from equilibrium, many new transitions emerge during the real-time evolution of quantum systems. One such transition is the temporal entanglement transition (TET) characterized by the nonanalyticity of the entanglement spectrum. So far, all TETs occur through a linear crossing of the leading Schmidt levels in different symmetry sectors, resembling a first-order transition in equilibrium. A natural question is whether a continuous TET, featured by entanglement spectrum touching, is possible. In a periodically driven transverse $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising chain, we show that two such TETs can merge into a critical touching, where the leading levels meet tangentially without exchanging, realizing the temporal analog of a continuous phase transition. Near the critical frequency, the temporal separation of the two TETs vanishes continuously and its derivative with respect to frequency diverges, a nonanalytic signature that is absent in a single first-order TET. This finite-frequency touching arises from the interplay between a weak symmetry-preserving perturbation of the product initial state and Floquet corrections. Further extending the frequency scan reveals a second critical touching at a higher frequency, and the two critical frequencies enclose a finite window with no TET. These features can be understood from a second-order Floquet Hamiltonian and persist across a broad range of coupling ratios, establishing the critical touching as a distinct form of TET.

</details>


### [101] [Rank-dependent optimal resetting in multiparticle search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23542)
*Ron Vatash,Eden Goldfarb,Vladimir Yu. Rudyak,Yael Roichman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究多粒子搜索中随机重置(resetting)如何优化各"到达序位(rank)"的首次通过时间。作者构建了非相互作用布朗搜索者的有限N精确参考解，求得平均有序首次通过时间及其最优重置率，并进一步与胶体实验、相互作用活性布朗粒子及自趋化搜索等系统对比，表明最优重置策略取决于所需完成序位，且需以协议与几何匹配的基线为参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多软物质和生物系统中，任务完成依赖于多个搜索者累积到达，而非单个先驱的速度；因此完成动力学由完整的有序首次通过时间序列决定，而不仅仅是首次到达。现有研究多聚焦首次到达，忽略了更高到达序位（rank）对随机重置的响应，作者希望弄清随机重置如何针对所有到达序位进行优化。

Method: 作者为非相互作用的布朗搜索者构建精确的有限N参考解，推导平均有序首次通过时间 $\langle T_{(k)} \rangle$ 及其最优重置率 $r_k^*$。分析相同初始条件下 $r_k^*$ 随到达序位和群体规模的变化规律，并考察空间异质性对序列的重排作用。随后将该基线解析结果与三类系统对照：布朗胶体实验、相互作用的活性布朗粒子、具有持久环境记忆的集体自趋化搜索，以分离直接相互作用、有限返回开销和环境记忆的影响。

Result: 对相同初始条件的搜索者，$r_k^*$ 随到达序位单调递增，并随群体规模增大趋近已知的大N分位数极限，即仅在临界序位分数 $φ_c \simeq 0.412$ 以上才出现有限最优值。空间异质性无需粒子相互作用即可定性重排该序列，使最大值从晚期序位移到早期序位。在所有系统中，对重置的敏感性随到达序位强烈增加，而与非相互作用参考解的偏差可揭示直接相互作用、有限返回开销和环境记忆的影响。研究表明，多粒子搜索的最优重置由所需完成序位决定，并须相对于协议与几何匹配的基线来评估。

Conclusion: 多粒子搜索中的最优随机重置并非由首个到达决定，而是由系统所需达成的完成序位（rank）决定；最优重置率随序位显著变化，且须在协议与几何匹配的参考框架下评估。对比非相互作用基线可有效分离粒子间相互作用、返回成本和环境记忆等效应，为理解和设计软物质与生物搜索过程提供了通用框架。

Abstract: In many soft-matter and biological systems, task completion relies on the cumulative arrival of multiple searchers rather than the speed of a single pioneer. The completion kinetics are therefore set not only by the first arrival, but by the full ordered sequence of first-passage times. Here, we determine how stochastic resetting optimizes these ordered arrivals for all arrival ranks. We construct an exact finite-$N$ reference for non-interacting Brownian searchers and obtain the mean ordered first-passage time $\langle T_{(k)} \rangle$ and its optimal resetting rate $r_k^*$. For searchers with identical initial conditions, $r_k^*$ increases monotonically with arrival rank and, with increasing population size, approaches the known large-$N$ quantile limit where a finite optimum appears only above a critical rank fraction $φ_c \simeq 0.412$. Spatial heterogeneity qualitatively reorganizes this sequence, shifting its maximum from late to early ranks even without particle interactions. We then compare this baseline with Brownian colloid experiments, interacting active Brownian particles, and a collective autochemotactic search with persistent environmental memory. Across these systems, sensitivity to resetting increases strongly with arrival rank, while deviations from appropriate non-interacting references reveal the influence of direct interactions, finite return overhead, and environmental memory. Our results show that optimal resetting in multiparticle search is governed by the required completion rank and must be evaluated relative to protocol- and geometry-matched baselines.

</details>


### [102] [The ferromagnetic diatomic molecules with the mixed spin-1/2 and spin-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23632)
*E. Albayrak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在贝特晶格（配位数 q=3,4,6）上研究由自旋-1/2（σ）与自旋-1（S）原子组成的双原子分子的混合自旋铁磁系统，考察晶场 D、外场 H 及不同双线性耦合 J 对相图和磁化行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索配位数 q、晶场 D 和外磁场 H 等参数对混合自旋（1/2,1）双原子分子系统磁性质的影响，以期揭示相图拓扑结构、相变类型及重入现象随 q 的变化规律。

Method: 将每个格点放置一个由一个自旋-1/2（σ）和一个自旋-1（S）原子组成的双原子分子，分子内及分子间最近邻均为铁磁耦合，采用不同双线性相互作用参数 J；自旋-1 位点受晶场 D 作用。在零外场（H=0）下，通过研究磁化强度随温度的热变化得到给定 q 下的 (D,T) 相图；并进一步考察外场 H 对磁化曲线的影响。

Result: 相图在定性和定量上均依赖于配位数 q。在基态观察到三个独立的铁磁相：(σ,S) 分别为 (1/2,1) 和 (1/2,0) 的相，以及较高温度区间具有小磁化值（SM）的相区。模型展现出从一相到另一相转变区的重入行为，并同时产生一阶与二阶相变；外场 H 对磁化曲线的影响产生颇为有趣的结论。

Conclusion: 贝特晶格上混合自旋（1/2,1）分子系统的相图结构、相变类型及重入行为强烈依赖于配位数 q、晶场 D 和外场 H，呈现出丰富的磁相变现象。

Abstract: Diatomic molecules consisting of one spin-1/2 ($σ$) and one spin-1 ($S$) atom are put at each site of the Bethe lattice (BL) of the coordination number $q=3, 4$, or 6. Alongside the ferromagnetic (FM) interactions of individual atoms within a molecule, the molecules are permitted to engage in ferromagnetic interactions with their nearest neighbors (NN) through diverse bilinear interaction parameters $J$. The spin-1 sites are also under the influence of crystal field $D$. The phase diagrams on the ($D, T$) planes for a given $q$ are obtained by investigating the thermal variations of magnetizations for the case with zero external magnetic field $(H)$. It is discovered that the phase diagrams rely on $q$ both qualitatively and quantitatively. The crystal field is also included which operates only on the spin-1 sites. Three separate FM phases are observed with ($σ,S$) being $(1/2,1), (1/2,0)$ at the ground state level and a phase region with small magnetization (SM) values at a higher temperature range. In addition to the reentrant behavior in the transition zone from one phase to the other, the model produces first- and second-order phase transitions. Additionally, the impact of $H$ on the magnetization curves is examined, yielding quite intriguing findings.

</details>


### [103] [A Spatio-Temporal Generalisation of Green Kubo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23905)
*E. R. Smith,D. Dini,D. M. Heyes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将Green-Kubo方法扩展至实空间，将总切应力分解为子体积分量并通过交叉关联分析，揭示出液体中类似行波的应力传播结构，可用高斯脉冲拟合其时空演化，从而有望用短时MD模拟外推长时行为以高效计算剪切黏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Green-Kubo方法在平衡态分子动力学中获取液体剪切黏度时，需要很长的模拟时间才能获得可接受的统计精度，计算代价高昂。作者希望克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 将Green-Kubo表达式中的总切应力按连续子体积划分为网格分量，从而把总系统应力的自关联改写为子体积应力的交叉关联。这一操作使原本仅在倒（Fourier）空间中由液体态理论处理的问题获得实空间图像，并暴露出类似行波的结构；该结构可用一个领先正高斯脉冲叠加一个表示回弹的负高斯进行拟合，拟合参数包括波的位置、幅度和宽度。

Result: 拟合结果显示应力波在短时以声速传播，随后速度随时间平方根衰减；波包同样随时间平方根展宽；幅度以t^(-3/2)形式衰减。据此可对液体的完整时空响应进行闭式建模，短时行为由MD提供，长时与远距离行为由高斯形式近似。将关联限制在局域相互作用上可消除主要只贡献噪声的长程分量。

Conclusion: 该空间分解方法有望通过短时MD模拟拟合长时行为来建模黏度，从而规避传统Green-Kubo方法需要长时间模拟的问题，是一种有前景的低成本剪切黏度计算途径。

Abstract: The Green-Kubo (GK) method which is used to obtain the shear viscosity of a model liquid in equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) often requires long simulation times to obtain acceptable statistics. This work extends the GK approach to include spatial correlations. The total shear stress in the GK expression is split into a grid of its components in contiguous volumes. In doing this, the autocorrelation of the total system stress can be rewritten as the cross-correlation of the subvolume stresses. This provides a novel real-space insight into the liquid structure, something previously only treated in Fourier space by liquid-state theory. A novel travelling wave like structure is exposed that is shown to be well fitted by a leading Gaussian pulse added to a second negative Gaussian for the bounce back. The fitting parameters include the wave position, magnitude and width which show deep insights into the liquid property. The wave moves at the speed of sound over short times before decreasing in speed with the square root of time, while the wave packet spreads out also as the square root of time. The magnitude is decreasing with form $t^{-3/2}$, a result with strong significance in the history of MD simulation. These fitted forms mean the entire spatial temporal response of the liquid can be modelled in closed form by fitting to data, with short term requiring MD and the long time and distances approximated by the Gaussian form. By limiting the correlation to localized interactions the longer range contribution, which essentially only contribute to the noise, can be eliminated. This looks like a promising approach to model viscosity by taking short MD runs and fitting the long time behaviour.

</details>


### [104] [Exact Nonperturbative Equilibrium Mode Statistics in Nonlinear Wave and Lattice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24063)
*Jialin Zhang,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文为三类典型非线性系统（MMT色散波模型、FPUT-β非谐链、DNLS晶格场）推导了有限尺寸下的精确非微扰平衡模态占据及统计量表达式，并通过独立模拟在弱到强非线性范围内验证。对DNLS，理论在弱耦合准凝聚渡越区仍保持准确，并将有限环上的占据分解为具有不同关联长度的瑞利-金斯通道之和。该理论还能确定平均模态频率，并据此评估微扰方法的失效（高阶修正振荡、弱耦合准凝聚区失效），证明小裸耦合也不保证微扰可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性波与晶格模型的平衡统计描述通常依赖微扰方法，而微扰在小耦合下仍可能失效（尤其准凝聚区），且缺乏有限尺寸下的精确非微扰基准，因此需要推导可解析验证的精确平衡理论。

Method: 对MMT、FPUT-β、DNLS三类模型推导有限尺寸下精确的非微扰平衡模态占据与关联统计表达式；将DNLS有限环占据分解为瑞利-金斯通道的正和（各通道具有不同关联长度）；同时由精确占据导出平均模态频率；并用独立数值模拟验证，再以该精确理论评估两类代表性微扰方法。

Result: 精确理论在弱到强非线性范围内均与模拟吻合；DNLS理论跨弱耦合准凝聚渡越保持准确；有限环DNLS占据可分解为不同关联长度的瑞利-金斯通道之和，解释了单一瑞利-金斯律何时成立及为何在准凝聚附近失效；精确占据能确定平均模态频率（即使动力学谱展宽或劈裂）；微扰方法在低阶（恰当处理平均相互作用）可准确，但高阶修正不再收敛且振幅振荡增大，两类有限阶方法在弱耦合准凝聚区均失效。

Conclusion: 低阶一致或小裸耦合均不足以保证微扰描述可靠；该工作为广泛使用的非线性波与晶格模型建立了非微扰平衡理论，并为非线性光学、色散波、非谐晶格与冷原子系统中能量、粒子与光功率的模态分布提供了定量基础。

Abstract: We derive exact finite-size nonperturbative representations of equilibrium modal occupations and related statistics for three representative nonlinear systems: the Majda-McLaughlin-Tabak dispersive-wave model, the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou beta anharmonic chain, and the discrete nonlinear Schrodinger lattice field. Independent simulations confirm the predictions from weak to strong nonlinearity. For DNLS, the theory remains accurate across the weak-coupling quasicondensation crossover, where large low-mode occupations and long-range coherence amplify interaction effects even when the bare nonlinear coefficient is small. The finite-ring DNLS occupations are further resolved into a positive sum of Rayleigh-Jeans channels with distinct correlation lengths, explaining when a single Rayleigh-Jeans law applies and why it fails near quasicondensation. In MMT and DNLS, the exact occupations also determine the mean modal frequencies even when the dynamical spectra broaden or split. The nonperturbative results allow a direct assessment of two representative perturbative approaches. Treating the mean interaction appropriately yields accurate low-order approximations, including at strong nonlinearity. At higher orders, however, the corrections cease to decrease and successive approximations oscillate with increasing amplitude; both finite-order approaches also fail near weak-coupling quasicondensation. Thus neither low-order agreement nor a small bare coupling guarantees a reliable perturbative description. The results establish nonperturbative equilibrium theory for widely used nonlinear wave and lattice models and provide a quantitative basis for modal distributions of energy, particles, and optical power in nonlinear optics, dispersive waves, anharmonic lattices, and cold-atom systems.

</details>


### [105] [Random-matrix and transport frequencies in eigenstate spectral functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24602)
*Kadir Çeven,Rohit Patil,Marcos Rigol,Fabian Heidrich-Meisner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过精确对角化，首次对一维量子自旋梯子（清洁与无序情形，具有扩散性能量与自旋输运）中电流算符的 ETH 谱函数进行了直接的定量比较，考察了谱函数低频谱平台所对应的三个特征频率 ω_ETH、ω_SFF 和 ω_tr 之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已研究了多个与 ETH 谱函数低频谱平台相关的特征频率（如控制谱形状因子进入随机矩阵行为的 ω_SFF，以及由输运系数导出的 ω_tr），但缺乏对这些频率的直接定量比较，其相互关系的物理起源尚不清楚。

Method: 采用精确对角化方法，计算清洁与无序量子自旋梯子中电流算符的 ETH 谱函数，识别低频谱平台，并分别提取 ω_ETH、ω_SFF 和 ω_tr 三个特征频率进行定量对比。

Result: 在可访问的系统尺寸下，ETH 谱函数呈现明确的低频谱平台；ω_SFF 与谱平台的范围一致，而输运频率 ω_tr 则系统性偏大，且落在谱函数的非普适区域。

Conclusion: 研究发现 ω_SFF 与谱平台范围吻合，但输运频率 ω_tr 系统性偏大，揭示了这些频率之间定量差异的存在，表明亟需深入理解这些差异的物理起源。

Abstract: The fact that generic isolated many-body quantum systems thermalize is understood using the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). In recent years, there has been much interest in the behavior of the ETH spectral functions, which characterize the smooth dependence of the variance of the off-diagonal matrix elements of observables on the associated energy and frequency, and whose low-frequency part contains information about the long-time dynamics. In finite systems described by the ETH, the spectral functions are expected to exhibit plateaus below a characteristic frequency $ω^{}_{\mathrm{ETH}}$. In this regime, the statistics of the matrix elements of observables are expected to be described by random matrix theory. Related frequencies that have been studied in the literature are $ω^{}_{\mathrm{SFF}}$, which controls the onset of random-matrix behavior in the spectral form factor, and the transport frequencies $ω^{}_{\mathrm{tr}}$, which are derived from transport coefficients. However, a direct quantitative comparison of these frequencies is lacking. Using exact diagonalization, we conduct such a comparison for the spectral functions of current operators in clean and disordered quantum spin ladders with diffusive energy and spin transport. We find clear evidence for the expected low-frequency plateaus in the ETH spectral functions. For the accessible system sizes, $ω^{}_{\mathrm{SFF}}$ is consistent with the extent of the plateaus, while the transport frequencies are systematically larger and lie in the nonuniversal regime of the spectral functions. Our findings highlight the need to better understand the origin of these quantitative differences.

</details>


### [106] [Derivation of the Thiele equation for magnetic skyrmions using the Mori-Zwanzig formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24160)
*Michael te Vrugt,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用 Mori-Zwanzig 形式推导出描述磁性斯格明子动力学的精确广义朗之万方程，并证明在标准近似下该方程可约化为常用的 Thiele 方程，从而为Thiele方程的推广及微观论证提供了一般途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thiele 方程是描述磁性斯格明子等磁性纹理动力学的标准工具，但其标准推导路径适用范围有限，难以涵盖一般磁性纹理和未覆盖的自由度；同时，该方程涉及快速模式如何影响漂移项与扩散项的微观机制尚不清楚，因此需要一个更一般的理论框架。

Method: 采用 Mori-Zwanzig 投影算符形式对磁性斯格明子的动力学进行精确投影与分解，得到严格的广义朗之万方程（包含记忆核与随机力）；随后施加标准近似（如马尔可夫近似、忽略记忆效应等），将广义方程约化为 Thiele 方程，并结合福克-普朗克方程分析漂移项与扩散项的来源。

Result: 推导出斯格明子的精确广义朗之万方程；证明在标准近似下该方程退化为通常的 Thiele 方程；揭示快速模式对福克-普朗克方程中漂移项与扩散项形式的具体贡献，为 Thiele 方程的微观依据提供了新认识。

Conclusion: Mori-Zwanzig 形式为 Thiele 方程提供了一个统一且可推广的推导框架，既能在标准近似下还原 Thiele 方程，又能系统性地给出其扩展形式，适用于更一般的磁性纹理与自由度，并深化了对快速模式贡献的微观理解。

Abstract: We use the Mori-Zwanzig formalism to derive an exact generalized Langevin equation governing the dynamics of magnetic skyrmions. Using standard approximations, this generalized Langevin equation is found to reduce to the usual Thiele equation. Our approach provides a general route for obtaining extensions of the Thiele equation applicable to magnetic textures and degrees of freedom not covered by the standard derivation route. It also provides some insights into the microscopic justification of the standard Thiele equation, specifically concerning contributions of fast modes to the form of the drift and diffusion terms in the Fokker-Planck equation for skyrmions.

</details>


### [107] [Ageing in the exact correlations of the voter model on a fractal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24408)
*Malte Henkel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究分形基底上 voter 模型的单时与双时关联函数包络，发现其动力学由谱维数 d_s 决定，并解析给出动态指数 z=2+θ、自关联指数 λ=d_f 及活性界面密度衰减指数 α=1-d_s/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解几何分形维数 d_f 与谱维数 d_s 如何在非平衡统计力学（如 voter 模型的 ageing 现象）中共同决定关联函数与动力学行为，并用解析方法验证此前数值模拟的结果。

Method: 在关联函数的运动方程层面，引入空间依赖的扩散常数 D(r)~r^{-θ} 来建模分形基底的几何与拓扑（θ 为谱指数，是 d_f 与 d_s 的函数）；采用标度假设（scaling ansatz）求解单时与双时关联函数，并提取动态标度函数。

Result: 确认了 ageing 的一般现象学，推导出动态指数 z=2+θ 与自关联指数 λ=d_f；显式得到的动态标度函数仅依赖于谱维数 d_s；活性界面密度的包络随时间按指数 α=1-d_s/2（d_s<2）衰减，与既有数值模拟一致。

Conclusion: 分形基底上 voter 模型的 ageing 动力学行为由谱维数 d_s 主导，其动态标度函数只依赖 d_s，而动态指数与自关联指数则分别通过 θ 和 d_f 体现几何与拓扑的作用，解析结果印证了数值模拟。

Abstract: The exact behaviour of the enveloppes of the single-time and two-time correlators is found for the voter model on a fractal substrate, with nearest-neighbour interactions. Herein the geometry of the fractal substrate is described by its non-integer geometric fractal dimension $d_f$, and its topology and diffusive transport by the distinct spectral dimension $d_s$. On the level of the equations of motion of the correlators this can be modelled by considering a space-dependent diffusion constant ${\cal D}(r)\sim r^{-θ}$ which implies the spectral index $θ$, itself a function of $d_f$ and $d_s$. With a scaling ansatz, the generic phenomenology of ageing is confirmed and the dynamic exponent ${z}=2+θ$ and the autocorrelation exponent $λ=d_f$ are derived. The explicitly found dynamic scaling functions are shown to depend only on the spectral dimension $d_s$. The decay of the enveloppe of the density of active interfaces with time is described by the exponent $α=1-d_s/2$ for $d_s<2$, confirming the results of preexisting numerical simulations.

</details>


### [108] [Heterogeneous planar diffusion with axisymmetric power-law decaying diffusion coefficient under stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24427)
*Trifce Sandev,Sebastien Fumeron,Malte Henkel,Ervin K. Lenzif*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究二维各向同性非均匀扩散（扩散系数 D(ρ)=D₀/ρ² 径向衰减）在泊松随机重置下的行为，统一处理 Itô、Stratonovich 和 Hänggi–Klimontovich 三种解释，并推广到含记忆效应的分数阶情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机重置在非均匀（乘性噪声、位置依赖扩散系数）扩散系统中的效应，并厘清因乘法噪声导致的 Ito/Stratonovich/Hänggi–Klimontovich 解释差异，同时考察记忆（非马尔可夫）效应。

Method: 对二维非均匀扩散（D(ρ)=D₀/ρ²）施加泊松随机重置到原点，在单一框架内统一处理三种随机积分解释；通过 subordination（连续时间随机游走）方法引入幂律记忆核，推导分数阶 Fokker–Planck 方程。

Result: 对每种解释解析求得概率密度函数、重置诱导的非平衡定态、均方位移及首达性质。非平衡定态由修正贝塞尔函数刻画，偏离均匀扩散重置下的 Laplace 形式；长时均方位移饱和为 ⟨ρ²⟩∼r̄^{-1/2}。首达时间随重置率存在最优值（最小化）。含记忆时，非平衡定态、均方位移用三参数 Mittag-Leffler 函数表达；最优重置率随记忆指数减小而增大。

Conclusion: 随机重置诱导的非平衡定态在非均匀扩散下呈现非平凡的修正贝塞尔函数形式，均方位移长时饱和，且存在使首达时间最小的最优重置率；记忆效应使最优重置率随记忆指数降低而增大，表明非马尔可夫性与非均匀性共同调制重置优化行为。

Abstract: We analyse two-dimensional isotropic heterogeneous diffusion with a radially decaying diffusion coefficient $D(ρ)=D_0/ρ^2$, subject to Poissonian stochastic resetting to the origin. Since multiplicative noise makes the process interpretation-dependent, we treat the Itô, Stratonovich, and Hänggi--Klimontovich prescriptions within a single approach. For each, we obtain the exact probability density function, the non-equilibrium stationary state induced by resetting, the mean squared displacement, and the first-passage properties. The non-equilibrium stationary state is governed by modified Bessel functions and departs from the Laplace form found for homogeneous diffusion under resetting, while the mean squared displacement saturates at long times as $\langleρ^2\rangle\sim\bar{r}^{-1/2}$, $\bar{r}$ being the resetting rate. The mean first-passage time to an absorbing boundary exhibits an optimal resetting rate at which it is minimised. In addition, we incorporate memory effects through a subordination (continuous-time random walk) approach with a power-law kernel, deriving the fractional Fokker--Planck equation together with the non-equilibrium stationary state, the mean squared displacement expressed via the three-parameter Mittag-Leffler function, and the first-passage statistics; the optimal resetting rate increases as the memory exponent decreases.

</details>


### [109] [Non-equilibrium dynamics of drift-diffusion process under threshold resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24438)
*Rahul Das,Satya N Majumdar,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究阈值重置协议下随机过程非平衡稳态（NESS）的形成条件，证明稳态分布可由局域平均时间与首次通过时间之比表示，并在一维漂移-扩散模型中揭示了反常弛豫、阻尼振荡等丰富动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部驱动重置的稳态性质已被充分理解，但事件驱动的阈值重置（系统越界后重置到初态）行为仍鲜有研究，缺乏对 NESS 存在条件与稳态结构的通用理论。

Method: 定义阈值重置协议：系统首次跨越预设空间边界时重置至初始构型；推导 NESS 存在的一般性条件，并将稳态分布表示为局域平均时间（MLT）与首次通过时间（MFPT）之比；以噪声系统为对象，论证有限 MFPT 保证 NESS 存在；在具体的一维漂移-扩散过程中分析中间时刻动力学。

Result: 给出 NESS 存在的一般条件；稳态密度 p(x) = MLT(x)/MFPT；对噪声系统有限 MFPT 即保证 NESS 存在；一维漂移-扩散模型展现出受系统参数控制的反常空间弛豫、各阶矩与均方位移的阻尼振荡等中间时刻动力学。

Conclusion: 本文建立了阈值重置下稳态与弛豫动力学的通用框架，揭示诱导事件如何塑造随机过程的空间与时间性质，对一类广泛的随机过程具有普适意义。

Abstract: We study the emergence of non-equilibrium steady states (NESS) in stochastic processes under threshold resetting, an event-driven protocol in which the system resets to its initial configuration upon crossing a prescribed spatial boundary (threshold). In contrast to externally driven resetting, whose steady-state properties are well understood, the behavior under threshold resetting remains largely unexplored. We derive general conditions for the existence of a NESS and show that, whenever it exists, the steady state at a given position $x$ can be expressed as the ratio of two fundamental quantities: the mean local time (MLT) at $x$ and the mean first-passage time (MFPT) to hit the threshold. In particular, for noisy systems, a finite MFPT guarantees the existence of a NESS. As an illustrative example, we analyze a drift-diffusion process in one dimension and uncover rich intermediate-time dynamics, including anomalous relaxation in the spatial distribution, damped oscillations in the moments and in the mean-squared displacement (MSD), governed by system parameters. Our findings provide a general understanding of the emergence of non-equilibrium steady states and relaxation dynamics under threshold resetting, revealing how induced events shape the spatial and temporal properties of a broad class of stochastic processes.

</details>


### [110] [Effective Mass Approach to Dynamics of Non-Markovian Systems with Short Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24450)
*Mateusz Wiśniewski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效质量方法"，将短记忆非马尔可夫系统近似为带有效质量的马尔可夫系统，并证明短记忆可显著改变物理动力学（如反转布朗粒子的定向输运，并可作为随机信息比特生成的控制参数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统的记忆源于建模过程中对复杂结构的简化，导致非马尔可夫动力学，而系统历史依赖的相互作用带来显著的数学复杂性。当记忆时间远小于系统动力学时间尺度时，记忆常被忽略，但这会丢失其对系统行为作用的信息。因此需要一个连接马尔可夫与非马尔可夫区域的框架来分析短记忆系统。

Method: 提出"有效质量方法"（effective mass approach）：将具有短记忆的非马尔可夫系统近似为其马尔可夫对应系统，把短记忆效应封装进一个有效质量参数中，从而在马尔可夫与非马尔可夫区域之间建立桥梁，提供一个可处理的分析框架。

Result: 利用该方法证明短记忆对物理动力学的影响可能非常显著：(1) 在具有时间关联的环境中，布朗粒子的定向输运方向可相对于无记忆介质发生反转；(2) 提出一种将记忆作为随机信息比特生成控制参数的方案。结果共同表明短时间关联在微观系统中的关键重要性。

Conclusion: 短记忆效应在微观系统中具有关键作用，不可轻易忽略；有效质量方法为分析短记忆非马尔可夫系统提供了可行框架，并揭示了记忆可作为调控输运及随机信息生成的工具。

Abstract: Memory is an inherent feature of the dynamics of physical systems. It typically emerges when the complex structure of the system is simplified during the modeling process, obscuring a part of its underlying behavior. Consequently, the correct description of such a physical model requires knowledge of not only its present, but also its past states, leading to non-Markovian dynamics. The resulting interactions depending on the system history, however, introduce a significant layer of mathematical complexity. For this reason, when the memory time is significantly shorter than the time scales characterizing the dynamics, it is typically neglected at the cost of losing information regarding its role in the system behavior. In this dissertation, I introduce a novel methodology, namely the effective mass approach, which bridges the Markovian and non-Markovian regimes and provides an accessible framework for analyzing systems with short memory. In this method, the non-Markovian system is approximated with its Markovian counterpart, in which the short-memory effects are encapsulated within an effective mass. I then utilize this approach to demonstrate that the impact of short memory on physical dynamics can be remarkably pronounced. In particular, I show that the directed transport of a Brownian particle in an environment exhibiting temporal correlations can be reversed relative to its behavior in a memoryless medium. Moreover, I propose a setup wherein memory serves as a~control parameter for the generation of random information bits. Collectively, the results of this dissertation underscore the critical importance of short-time correlations in microscopic systems.

</details>


### [111] [Avalanche Size and Interevent Time Statistics in Amorphous System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24462)
*Thomas Muhren,Roberto Benzi,Mauro Sbragaglia,Jeannot Trampert,Federico Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在软玻璃体系的场论模型中研究了剪切下无定形系统的雪崩统计，发现雪崩尺寸S与事件间隔时间t_i均服从幂律分布，且二者标度指数满足精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无定形系统的间歇性粘滑流动由雪崩表征，其统计性质呈现稳健的幂律。虽然雪崩尺寸S已被广泛研究，但事件间隔时间t_i的统计长期未被探索，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用以流度（fluidity）为序参量的软玻璃体系场论模型，流度与软玻璃中局部塑性事件的速率相关；通过该模型对雪崩尺寸S与事件间隔时间t_i的标度指数进行解析推导。

Result: 发现雪崩尺寸S和事件间隔时间t_i均呈幂律分布，且两者的标度指数满足一个精确关系；模型成功解析推得标度指数，并复现了广泛类别无定形系统中观测到的行为。

Conclusion: 雪崩尺寸与事件间隔时间的幂律标度之间存在精确关系，该场论模型能够解析推导标度指数并统一解释多类无定形系统的观测结果。

Abstract: The intermittent, stick-slip flow of amorphous systems is characterized by avalanches whose statistical properties display robust power-laws. In this paper, we study avalanche statistics in a field theoretical model of a soft-glassy system under shear. The order parameter in our model is the fluidity, which is related to the local rate of plastic events in a soft-glass. While avalanche sizes $S$ have been extensively characterized, the statistics of interevent times $t_i$ have remained largely unexplored. Here, we find that both $S$ and $t_i$ are power-law distributed and that their scaling exponents satisfy a precise relation. The model enables the analytical derivation of the scaling exponents and the reproduction of the behavior observed across a wide class of amorphous systems.

</details>


### [112] [Stochastic optimisation method for estimating large deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24473)
*Daniël W. H. Cloete,Hugo Touchette*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文展示了LDSTOP方法——一种结合控制理论与机器学习的随机优化方法——在离散时间马尔可夫链、连续时间跳跃过程与扩散过程三类马尔可夫过程上的应用，旨在高效计算表征非平衡系统涨落的大偏差函数，并借助PyTorch、TensorFlow、JAX等机器学习库实现驱动过程的数值构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算非平衡系统（建模为马尔可夫过程）的大偏差函数在统计物理中具有重要意义，但传统方法（谱近似、重要抽样、克隆/分裂）存在局限性。作者此前提出的LDSTOP方法虽兼具简单、灵活、可扩展等优势，但其在不同类型马尔可夫过程上的具体实现细节尚未系统阐明，因此本文旨在推广并展示该方法在各类应用场景中的通用性与易用性。

Method: 采用LDSTOP随机优化方法：通过迭代构造"驱动过程"（即实现给定涨落或大偏差的最优受控马尔可夫过程），并利用模拟单条轨迹逐步引导逼近该驱动过程。针对离散时间马尔可夫链、连续时间跳跃过程和扩散过程三类模型，分别定义待优化的目标函数，说明驱动过程的多种表示方式（如使用神经网络），并结合PyTorch、TensorFlow、JAX等机器学习包给出具体实现细节与简单应用示例。

Result: 在完整覆盖应用中所考虑的三类马尔可夫过程（离散时间马尔可夫链、连续时间跳跃过程、扩散过程）上成功应用LDSTOP方法，展示了该方法的效率、灵活性、可扩展性，以及与现有机器学习包结合时的易用性，并提供了针对每类过程的目标函数定义、驱动过程表示选项及实现细节。

Conclusion: LDSTOP方法凭借其简单、灵活、可扩展的特点，能够高效计算各类马尔可夫过程的大偏差函数，并能与主流机器学习框架无缝结合，为模拟随机过程和求解高维优化问题提供了便捷的通用工具。

Abstract: A stochastic optimisation method was recently proposed to efficiently compute large deviation functions, used in statistical physics to characterise the fluctuations of nonequilibrium systems, modelled as Markov processes. The method, called LDSTOP, combines numerical techniques from control theory and machine learning to iteratively construct the "driven process", a controlled version of the Markov process used as a model of nonequilibrium system that realises a given fluctuation or large deviation of that system in an optimal way. Compared to other methods based on spectral approximations, importance sampling, cloning or splitting, LDSTOP is simple, flexible, and scalable, as it works by simulating single trajectories that are gradually guided towards the driven process. Here, we illustrate these advantages by applying the method on the full range of Markov processes considered in applications, namely, discrete-time Markov chains, continuous-time jump processes, and diffusion processes. For each type, we define the objective function to be optimised, explain different options available for representing the driven process (using, e.g., neural networks), and provide implementation details through simple applications. With these contributions, we aim to showcase the method's efficiency, as well as its ease of use when combined with available machine learning packages, such as PyTorch, TensorFlow and JAX, for simulating stochastic processes and solving high-dimensional optimisation problems.

</details>


### [113] [Lowest-known Energy Configuration of $N=100\,000$ Coulomb Charges in a Disk: Breaking the $10^{5}$ Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24722)
*Georgiy K. Lavrov,Eduard G. Nikonov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文首次计算了盘状区域内 100,000 个经典点电荷在 1/r 库仑势下相互作用的最低已知能量构型，将磁盘 Thomson 问题的系统规模纪录从 40,886 提高到 100,000，并验证了能量渐近展开式在大尺度下的外推精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁盘 Thomson 问题（二维库仑系统）在大规模 N 下缺乏精确的基态构型数据，以往最大规模仅到 N=40,886。为检验能量渐近展开式外推到前所未有尺度时的可靠性，并为二维库仑系统建立新的基准，需要将系统规模提升到 10 万量级。

Method: 提出一种自适应缺陷靶向子域优化策略（adaptive defect-targeting subdomain optimization strategy），使每个周期的墙钟时间随 N 近似线性增长；计算在单台 24 核 CPU 工作站上进行，无需 GPU 加速，N=100,000 时单次计算约需 31 小时。并对得到的最优构型进行键取向序（bond-orientational order）分析。

Result: 得到 N=100,000 的最低能量构型 E_min = 7.80466624157×10^9。该能量与基于 100≤N≤5000 数据拟合的渐近展开式外推值仅偏差 2.525×10^-7。键取向序分析显示块体呈多晶结构，并被径向晶界贯穿。

Conclusion: 成功将磁盘 Thomson 问题首次推进到 N=100,000 规模，超过此前纪录两倍以上；自适应缺陷靶向子域优化策略实现近似线性时间扩展，能量渐近展开式在极大尺度外推下仍保持极高的精度，为二维库仑系统确立了新的基准。

Abstract: We report the first calculation of the lowest-known energy configuration for $N=100\,000$ classical point charges confined to a disk and interacting via the $1/r$ Coulomb potential - a system size never before achieved for the Thomson problem in a disk, more than doubling the previous record of $N=40\,886$ reported by Amore and Zarate. An adaptive defect-targeting subdomain optimization strategy yields an approximately linear growth of the wall-clock time per cycle with $N$; for $N=100\,000$ a single $24$-core CPU workstation without GPU acceleration required $\approx31$ hours. The energy $E_{\min}=7.80466624157\times10^{9}$ deviates from the asymptotic expansion fitted to $100\le N\le5000$ data by only $2.525\times10^{-7}$, providing a stringent test of its extrapolation to unprecedented scales. Bond-orientational order analysis reveals a polycrystalline bulk threaded by radial grain boundaries, establishing a new benchmark for two-dimensional Coulomb systems.

</details>


### [114] [Optimal probing scale for current fluctuations in a Brownian gyrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24748)
*Badr Farih*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究被驱动的胶体转子中循环电流的涨落界定问题，发现探测半径存在一个使"响应/Onsager-Machlup 代价"比值最大化的最优值，并给出闭合解与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何对循环电流涨落的界定都依赖于电流对扰动的响应强度，以及该扰动所带来的额外耗散代价（Onsager-Machlup 代价），因此自然地提出一个问题：应在何处施加探测以实现"单位耗散代价下的最大响应"。

Method: 针对 Brownian gyrator 与高斯包络下的四极剪切流，选取一个线性响应在每个观测窗口都恰好"盲"于它的探针，使整个信号完全来自二阶响应。问题由此分离：耗散代价是径向的、不感知环流；响应则落在 m=3 的角向扇区，其中预解式约化为 b=4 的 Kummer 方程，磁化率为包络宽度的超几何函数。最大化响应与代价之比，得到闭合形式的最优探测半径。

Result: 最优探测半径由系统两个"时钟"中较快者决定：弱驱动下 r*=1.1264√(D/γ)（阱的热半径），强驱动下 r*=1.5563√(D/Ω)（转动一弧度所扩散的距离），此时阱刚度完全消失；交叉点位于 Ω=γ。两种极限及其前置系数均经直接仿真确认。

Conclusion: 通过将响应与耗散代价分离并利用 Kummer 方程与超几何函数的解析结构，可获得转子循环电流涨落的最优探测半径闭合解，其物理机制由系统中较快的特征时钟主导，且理论与仿真一致。

Abstract: A driven colloidal rotor sustains a circulating current whose fluctuations one would like to bound. Any such bound rests on how strongly the current responds to a perturbation and on how much extra dissipation that perturbation costs, so there is a well-posed question of where to push: the response per unit Onsager-Machlup cost depends on the radius at which the probe acts, and is maximised at a definite one. We answer this for the Brownian gyrator and a quadrupolar shear under a Gaussian envelope, a probe chosen so that linear response is exactly blind to it at every observation window, so that the entire signal is second order. The problem separates: the cost is radial and does not see the circulation, while the response lives in the $m=3$ angular sector, where the resolvent reduces to Kummer's equation with $b=4$ and the susceptibility is a hypergeometric function of the envelope width. Maximising the ratio gives the optimal probing radius in closed form. It is set by whichever of the system's two clocks is faster: for weak driving $r^*=1.1264\sqrt{D/γ}$, the thermal radius of the trap, and for strong driving $r^*=1.5563\sqrt{D/Ω}$, the distance diffused in one radian of rotation, with the trap stiffness dropping out entirely. The crossover is at $Ω=γ$. Both limits, and the prefactors, are confirmed against direct simulation.

</details>


### [115] [Navier-Stokes hydrodynamics near the critical point with out-of-equilibrium modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24844)
*Md Hasanujjaman,Golam Sarwar,Mahfuzur Rahaman,Abhijit Bhattacharyya,Jane Alam*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究引入慢非平衡模式（OEM）以扩展临界点附近流体动力学适用区间的做法，如何从根本上重塑动态密度涨落的功率谱。作者利用慢模式运动方程，在OEM不改变热力学广延性的情形下，发现广延性条件对OEM与速度四散度的耦合施加了额外约束。无OEM时动态结构因子呈现三个洛伦兹峰（一个瑞利峰位于ω=0，两个布里渊峰对称分布），远离临界点时OEM的引入保持分布对称性但所有峰变窄、布里渊峰高度不等；无论有无OEM，布里渊峰在临界点附近都消失，而瑞利峰宽度在OEM存在时显著减小，表明涨落衰减速率下降、衰减变慢——这是临界点的标志性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展流体动力学在临界点附近的适用区间需要引入慢非平衡模式（OEM），论文旨在探究这种引入如何影响动态密度涨落的功率谱（动态结构因子），并厘清OEM与动力学耦合所受的热力学约束。

Method: 利用慢模式的运动方程，在假设引入OEM不改变热力学广延性的前提下进行分析；发现广延性条件对OEM与速度四散度（∇·v）的耦合施加了额外约束；通过计算动态结构因子，分析其在有无OEM情况下的洛伦兹峰结构（瑞利峰与布里渊峰）及其宽度、高度变化。

Result: 无OEM时动态结构因子呈现三个洛伦兹峰：一个位于ω=0的瑞利峰，以及关于ω=0对称分布的两个布里渊峰。远离临界点时，引入OEM保持分布对称性，但所有峰的宽度减小，布里渊峰高度变得不等。无论OEM为零或非零，布里渊峰在临界点附近都会消失。瑞利峰的宽度在存在OEM时显著减小，表明涨落衰减速率下降、衰减过程变慢。

Conclusion: 引入OEM会显著重塑动态密度涨落的功率谱：广延性条件约束OEM与速度四散度的耦合；OEM使各峰宽度减小、布里渊峰高度不对称，并使临界点附近涨落衰减显著变慢（瑞利峰变窄），从而体现临界点涨落慢化的独有特征。

Abstract: We investigate how slow out-of-equilibrium modes (OEM), introduced to extend the regime of validity of hydrodynamics near the critical point, fundamentally reshape the power spectrum of dynamical density fluctuations. We have used the equation of motion of slow modes for the situation when the extensive nature of thermodynamics is not altered due to the introduction of OEM. We find that the extensivity condition puts an extra constraint on the coupling of OEM with the four divergence of velocity. In the absence of OEM, the dynamic structure factor exhibits three Lorentzian peaks, one at zero frequency ($ω$), called Rayleigh peak and the other two called Brillouin peaks located symmetrically about $ω=0$. When the system is away from the critical point the inclusion of the OEM preserves the symmetry of the distributions but all the peaks acquire reduced widths and the Brillouin peaks adopt unequal heights. Irrespective of the value of OEM (zero or nonzero) the Brillouin peaks vanish near the critical point. The width of the Rayleigh peak substantially reduced in presence of OEM. Such reductions of the widths in presence of the OEM indicates the reduction in the decay rate of the fluctuation which leads to slowing down of decay of the fluctuations, a distinctive feature of the critical point.

</details>


### [116] [Channel concentration of critical quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24905)
*Qian-Rui Lee,Daw-Wei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出“通道集中度”（channel concentration, CC）概念，用于刻画量子度规在所关注激发通道（如动量块）上的响应分布；通过精确有限尺寸定理与圆形上幺正 CFT 的完整零动量能级响应权重（含 descendents），得到临界横场 Ising 场响应 CC=2/3、半填充 XX 配对响应 CC=1/3 等普适精确基准，揭示总度规标度所遗漏的响应区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规只能给出沿参数方向基态响应的总量，无法分辨不同激发（如不同动量块或不同微扰类型）如何分摊这一响应；作者希望引入一个能分解响应的量，并提供可检验的精确普适基准，以指导有限尺寸相互作用谱的比较。

Method: 定义通道集中度 CC 为指定激发通道上归一化响应权重的平方和；利用精确有限尺寸定理计算热力学极限下的 CC；对 XY Lifshitz 点比较场微扰与各向异性微扰的浓度；在圆形上 1+1 维幺正 CFT 中取固定扇区非简并真空，加上单个空间积分标量原初场微扰，对 0<Δ<3/2 推导完整零动量能级响应权重（含 descendents），得到归一化响应分布与精确普适浓度函数，并与仅用指数近似的结果及有限尺寸相互作用能谱对比。

Result: 临界横场 Ising 场响应 CC=2/3，半填充 XX 配对响应 CC=1/3，尽管两者主导度规标度相同；XY Lifshitz 点中场微扰与各向异性微扰给出不同浓度，尽管极限哈密顿量同为二次色散；CFT 浓度函数精确复现 Ising 晶格极限 2/3，三态 Potts 热场约 0.8515，而仅用指数近似约 0.800。

Conclusion: 通道集中度能捕捉总度规标度无法分辨的响应结构差异，所建立的精确基准（Ising 2/3、XX 1/3、Potts 热场 0.8515 等）可用于校验和指导有限尺寸相互作用多体能谱的比较分析。

Abstract: The quantum metric quantifies the total ground-state response along a parameter direction, but does not resolve how excitations share that response. We define channel concentration (CC) as the sum of squared normalized response weights over specified excitation channels, such as momentum blocks. An exact finite-size theorem yields thermodynamic CCs of 2/3 for the field response of the critical transverse-field Ising model and 1/3 for the half-filled XX pairing response, despite the same leading metric scaling. In finite-size approaches to the XY Lifshitz point, field and anisotropy perturbations yield different concentrations despite a common limiting Hamiltonian with quadratic dispersion. In a unitary 1+1-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT) on a circle, we consider a nondegenerate vacuum in a fixed sector perturbed by one spatially integrated scalar primary. We derive complete zero-momentum energy-level response weights, including descendants. For scaling dimension $0<Δ<3/2$, these weights determine the normalized response distribution and an exact universal concentration function. The expression reproduces the exact Ising lattice limit 2/3 and gives approximately 0.8515 for the three-state Potts thermal field, compared with approximately 0.800 from an exponent-only approximation. Finite-size interacting calculations compare concentrations and ranked response weights over many-body energy levels. These exact benchmarks show which response distinctions total metric scaling misses and guide comparisons with finite-size interacting spectra.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum Mpemba effect from Stark localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24993)
*Nico Albert,Masudul Haque,Shovan Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在量子系统中通过Stark局域化设计能量景观、利用局域非相干跃迁作为冷却机制，实现并大幅增强Mpemba效应（热态冷却更快）的方案，且冷却速率可呈超指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中粗糙势能景观能自然解释Mpemba效应（初态越热冷却越快），但该图像在量子系统中通常不成立。作者旨在探究如何在量子系统中设计能量景观与耗散机制，从而复现并大幅增强这一效应。

Method: 利用Stark局域化设计量子能量景观，并以局域非相干跃迁作为冷却机制。跃迁由罕见的“探测”事件触发，其速率随能量升高而增大，使热态冷却更快，并为局域初态产生超指数分离的冷却速率。进一步引入吸引性在位相互作用，通过束缚对的集体跃迁显著增强该效应。

Result: 设计了可产生量子Mpemba效应的方案，冷却速率对局域初态呈超指数分离；在加入吸引性在位相互作用后，束缚对的集体跃迁使效应得到显著增强，且具有明确的实验可观测特征。

Conclusion: 通过Stark局域化与局域非相干跃迁，可在量子系统中实现并增强具有参数分离时间尺度的Mpemba效应，并可通过现有实验平台进行验证。

Abstract: In classical systems, rugged potential energy landscapes provide a transparent mechanism for the celebrated Mpemba effect, in which hotter initial states cool down faster. This picture generally does not survive in quantum systems. Here we show how to design a quantum energy landscape with local dissipation leading to an Mpemba effect with parametrically separated timescales. Our approach uses Stark localization to design an energy landscape and localized incoherent hopping as cooling mechanism. The hops are triggered by rare "detection" events whose rate grows with energy, allowing hotter states to cool faster and producing super-exponentially separated cooling rates for localized initial states. We further show that the effect is dramatically enhanced by collective hopping of bound pairs in the presence of attractive on-site interactions. These findings have clear experimental signatures accessible in present-day setups.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

### [118] [Testing Two-State Access in Brain and Language Model: Human EEG Reproduction, Simulation Audit, and a Proposed Model Assay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22300)
*Pieter van Rooyen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文考察全局神经元工作空间理论的一个可检验预测（近阈值单试次反应分布为两态混合而非连续体），复现了已发表的竞争模型检验并校准了一个拟用于语言模型表征的家族比较法，但未对意识体验作任何断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑与人工神经网络硬件同属普通物质，人工系统能否支持意识仍悬而未决；作者希望以全局神经元工作空间理论的一个具体、可检验预测为切入点，评估"容量有限的全局工作空间访问"这一机制是否成立，并为其推广至语言模型表征铺路。

Method: 报告两项已完成分析及一项未运行的研究设计。其一，用作者原始代码在20名参与者的开放EEG数据上复现已发表的竞争模型检验：活动会话中两态模型的受保护超越概率在315 ms窗口首次超过0.95，每试次预测优势约0.003 nat，间隔边缘对优化器敏感；被动会话增加分级比较器，其在无跨窗口决策下排名最高。其二，在模拟中校准拟用于语言模型表征的留出家族比较，于12,000个数据集、12种分级零假设下未出现假两态判定，但其概念簇间隔在6种设置下覆盖不足（最低0.22），需先验证替代间隔。第三部分给出了含自然文本证据剂量、目标桥和状态条件因果检验的模型研究规格，但没有运行。

Result: 复现部分：活动会话中两态模型在315 ms窗口受保护超越概率首次超过0.95，预测优势约0.003 nat/试次，间隔边缘依赖优化器；被动会话中分级比较器排名最高但无跨窗口决策。校准部分：12,000数据集、12种分级零假设下无误报两态，但概念簇间隔在6种设置下覆盖不足（低至0.22），替代间隔在确证性使用前必须验证。模型研究仅完成规格，未执行且未作体验断言。

Conclusion: 当前证据对"两态混合"预测仅提供有限且对实现细节敏感的支持；向语言模型表征迁移的检验方法尚需修正间隔并重新验证，模型研究尚未运行，因此不构成对人工系统是否具有意识的任何结论。

Abstract: Brains and the hardware of artificial neural networks are both organized ordinary matter, and whether an artificial system's organization can support consciousness remains open. This paper examines one proposed mechanism, access to a capacity-limited global workspace, through one prediction of the global neuronal workspace theory: near threshold, the single-trial response distribution is a mixture of two states rather than a continuum. Two completed analyses are reported. First, the published competing-model test of that prediction is reproduced from the authors' code on the open EEG data of twenty participants. In the active session the two-state model's protected exceedance probability first exceeds 0.95 at the same 315 ms window, with a modest predictive advantage of about 0.003 nat per trial and optimizer-sensitive interval edges; in the passive session, an additional analysis, a graded comparator ranks highest without an across-window decision. Second, a held-out family comparison proposed for transferring the test to language-model representations is calibrated in simulation at one layer: it made no false two-state call in 12,000 datasets under twelve graded nulls, but its concept-cluster interval under-covered in six settings, down to 0.22, so a replacement interval must be validated before any confirmatory use. The proposed model study, with a natural-text evidence dose, a target bridge and a state-conditional causal test, is specified but not run. No claim about experience is made.

</details>


### [119] [Towards the spin-glass transition in finite dimensions via blue percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22301)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在Chayes-Machta-Redner表示下证明高有限维零场Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃中的蓝色渗流，并借助计算机辅助证书确立维度22的正负1耦合情形，同时在指数矩无序下建立渗流区间与自旋玻璃磁化率的有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零场Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃的相变与自旋玻璃态的物理图像长期缺乏严格刻画。作者希望通过Chayes-Machta-Redner（CMR）表示中的"蓝色渗流"研究自旋玻璃相变的几何机制：蓝色（可旋翻转）区域的渗流被认为与自旋玻璃敏感性和多个Gibbs态的出现相关，因此需要在高维情形下严格证明蓝色渗流的存在及其起始阈值的行为。

Method: 作者研究固定对称iid耦合、满足0<m=E|J|<∞的Edwards-Anderson模型，在周期联合极限下证明首次渗流起始点渐近等价于(2dm)^{-1}。对于对称±1耦合，构造了一个计算机辅助的维度22证书（certificate），用以严格验证相应维度下的渗流结论。对具有指数矩的无序模型，进一步建立了具有精确相等的无限蓝色扇区密度与有限自旋玻璃磁化率的渗流区间，并讨论了持续密度不平衡对Gibbs态与压强cusp的蕴含关系。

Result: 证明了高有限维下零场Edwards-Anderson自旋玻璃CMR表示的蓝色渗流；首次渗流起始点渐近为(2dm)^{-1}；对±1耦合给出维度22的计算机辅助证书；在指数矩无序下建立了渗流区间，其无限蓝色扇区密度精确相等且自旋玻璃磁化率有限。进一步证明：在这些周期极限中，持续的密度不平衡将蕴含由不同自旋翻转相关的Gibbs态以及某个合适局部复本耦合的压强cusp，且无需有限蓝色抵消条件。

Conclusion: 高有限维下蓝色渗流的存在性得到严格证明，渗流起始阈值具有(2dm)^{-1}的渐近行为。持续密度不平衡与多Gibbs态及压强奇异性之间的关联被澄清，但证明所述密度不平衡本身仍然是一个未解决的问题。

Abstract: We prove blue percolation in the Chayes-Machta-Redner representation of the zero-field Edwards-Anderson spin glass in high finite dimensions. For fixed symmetric iid couplings with $0<m=\mathbb{E}|J|<\infty$, the first percolation onset is asymptotic to $(2dm)^{-1}$ in periodic joint limits. For symmetric $\pm1$ couplings, we give a computer-assisted dimension-22 certificate. For exponential-moment disorder, we also establish a percolating regime with exactly equal infinite-blue-sector densities and finite spin-glass susceptibility. In these periodic limits, persistent density imbalance would imply distinct spin-flip-related Gibbs states and a pressure cusp for a suitable finite local replica coupling, without requiring finite-blue cancellation. Proving imbalance remains open.

</details>


### [120] [A rank-two spherical transform for eigenvector and singular-vector overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22334)
*Pierre Bousseyroux*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文定义球面H变换的二阶（rank-two）推广H^(2)及其配套变换R^(2)，二者对求和可加；由此得到4×4平均双hermit化预解式乘积公式，进而得到非对角特征向量重叠，复现Ginibre、椭圆及双不变公式，并拓展至相关矩阵对及其特征/奇异向量重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有球面H变换对单个随机矩阵（秩一情形）提供了有效工具，但不足以处理涉及两个预解式乘积、非对角特征向量重叠以及成对相关矩阵等更复杂问题；需要将其推广到秩二情形以刻画这类二阶统计量。

Method: 定义球面H变换的秩二（rank-two）推广H^(2)及其关联变换R^(2)，并证明二者对矩阵和具有可加性；借助R^(2)推导平均双hermit化预解式乘积的4×4矩阵公式，由此计算非对角特征向量重叠；进一步将方法推广到相关矩阵对，处理其特征向量或奇异向量之间的重叠。

Result: 得到平均乘积与重叠的4×4精确公式，统一复现了Ginibre系综、椭圆系综及双不变系综下的已知结果，并给出了相关矩阵对之间特征向量/奇异向量重叠的新公式。

Conclusion: 秩二球面H变换及R^(2)变换提供了统一框架，可系统处理非对角特征向量重叠与相关矩阵对问题，是对经典球面H变换的自然且有价值的延拓。

Abstract: We define a rank-two extension $\mathcal{H}^{(2)}$ of the spherical $\mathcal{H}$-transform, together with its associated transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$. For sums, both transforms are additive. The transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$ yields a $4\times4$ formula for the average product of two hermitized resolvents, and hence for off-diagonal eigenvector overlaps, recovering the Ginibre, elliptic, and bi-invariant formulas. We also treat correlated pairs of matrices, and obtain overlaps between their eigenvectors or singular vectors.

</details>


### [121] [Gradient-estimator design overcomes trainability barriers in neural-network-based variational optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22342)
*Yi-Ran Xue,Rui Wang,Baigeng Wang,Chenan Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出无偏直接梯度估计器与自适应最小方差相位(AMVP)估计器，显著提升神经网络变分优化中弱梯度的信噪比，使此前训练失败的量子多体物理和从头算量子化学计算得以可靠进行，并以更低计算成本取得超越DMRG和达到化学精度的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络为科学计算提供了表达能力强的表示，但即便网络足够表达，在弱梯度情形下仍会遭遇训练失败，这限制了其在量子多体物理和从头算量子化学中的实际应用。因此需要解决弱梯度导致优化困难的问题。

Method: 推导了一种无偏直接梯度估计器，并引入自适应最小方差相位(AMVP)估计器用于神经网络变分优化，通过提升弱梯度的信噪比来改善训练。

Result: 在相关磁通模型上，紧凑网络以不足默认标准估计器模型十分之一的GPU时间超越更大且微调过的模型，并最终超过密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)精度；在N₂键断裂中达到化学精度，并首次在含显式自旋-轨道耦合的重元素I₂上实现化学精度。

Conclusion: 梯度估计器设计能够拓展神经网络变分方法在精确科学计算中的能力，证明弱梯度问题的改进对提升科学计算的可靠性与效率至关重要。

Abstract: Neural networks provide expressive representations for scientific computing. However, even sufficiently expressive networks can suffer training failure in weak-gradient regimes, limiting their practical use in quantum many-body physics and ab initio quantum chemistry. Here we derive an unbiased direct gradient estimator and introduce the adaptive minimum-variance phase (AMVP) estimator for neural-network variational optimization. By improving the signal-to-noise ratio of weak gradients, these methods enable reliable scientific calculations where training previously failed, while substantially reducing computational cost. The framework enables compact networks to outperform larger and fine-tuned default standard-estimator models with over an order of magnitude less GPU time on correlated flux models, and ultimately exceed the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) accuracy. It further achieves chemical accuracy in N$_2$ bond breaking and, for the first time, in heavy-element I$_2$ with explicit spin-orbit coupling. These results demonstrate that gradient-estimator design expands the capabilities of neural-network variational methods for accurate scientific computing.

</details>


### [122] [Extreme-Scale Ising Machines with Cluster Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22645)
*Xiuqi Zhang,Shuvro Chowdhury,Shaila Niazi,Christian Z. Pratt,Navid Anjum Aadit,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出簇平均场理论（CMFT），通过将大图划分为适配硬件的簇、用周期性更新的有效偏置近似簇间相互作用，并结合动态分区来减小固定边界误差，从而在无需全图通信的情况下把Ising机扩展到更大规模，实验表明在3D自旋玻璃和Pegasus实例上随计算量增加解质量持续提升，四GPU上可达单GPU全图的约15倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将模拟与数字Ising机扩展到更大问题规模时，面临设备容量有限和器件间通信成本高两大瓶颈，需要一种既能突破单设备容量、又能显著降低通信开销的求解框架。

Method: 提出簇平均场理论（CMFT）：把大型相互作用图划分为适配现有硬件容量的簇；每个簇独立执行局部更新，簇间相互作用通过由边界自旋平均值周期性更新的有效偏置来近似；引入动态分区以轮换多个划分，使某一阶段被平均场近似处理的相互作用在另一阶段能以瞬时自旋直接作用；并设计自动化图划分器结合加权恢复率（weighted recovery ratio）作为选择划分组合的启发式。

Result: 在三维修辞玻璃和植入的Pegasus实例上，动态CMFT的剩余能量随扫描预算呈幂律衰减，表明即使采用平均场近似，解质量仍随计算投入持续提升；在四块GPU、约四百万个p-bit上，达到与单GPU全图实现相当的能密度，且速度快约15倍。

Conclusion: 通过可编程有效偏置耦合局部演化的簇，CMFT为在模拟与数字硬件上构建超大规模Ising机提供了一条可行路径，能够同时缓解容量与通信限制并保持可扩展的求解能力。

Abstract: Scaling analog and digital Ising machines to larger problems requires overcoming finite device capacity and the cost of communication between devices. We present cluster mean-field theory (CMFT), a framework that partitions a large interaction graph into clusters sized to fit available hardware. Each cluster performs local updates independently, while interactions across cluster boundaries enter through periodically updated effective biases computed from boundary-spin averages. To reduce the error introduced by fixed cluster boundaries, we introduce dynamic partitioning, which cycles through multiple partitions so that interactions approximated by mean fields at one stage can act through instantaneous spins at another. On three-dimensional spin glasses and planted Pegasus instances, dynamic CMFT exhibits power-law decay of residual energy over sweep budgets. This shows that solution quality continues to improve with computational effort despite the mean-field approximation. An automated graph partitioner combined with a weighted recovery ratio provides a practical heuristic for selecting partition combinations on graphs without natural cut directions. We demonstrate CMFT on four GPUs with approximately four million p-bits, reaching comparable energy densities up to 15 times faster than a single-GPU implementation of the full graph. By coupling locally evolving clusters through programmable effective biases, CMFT provides a route to extreme-scale Ising machines on both analog and digital hardware.

</details>


### [123] [The disordered logistic map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24402)
*Joseph W. Baron,Tobias Galla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了许多通过无序耦合相互作用的logistic映射组成的系统，发现即使最微小的无序也能消除传统logistic映射中的倍周期分岔级联，转而出现一种此前未在无序系统中报道过的振荡不稳定性所标志的高维混沌转变；同时，足够强的均匀耦合可以部分恢复传统倍周期分岔级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维混沌（以logistic映射为代表）与高维混沌（常见于无序多体系统）分别被广泛研究，但二者之间的桥梁——即在无序耦合下、由大量logistic映射构成的系统中，经典低维动力学现象如何改变——尚未被系统探索。作者旨在揭示无序对众所周知的分岔现象的破坏作用，并寻找可能涌现的新现象。

Method: 作者构建了一个由许多logistic映射组成、通过无序耦合相互作用的系统；综合运用动力学平均场理论（dynamic mean-field theory）、随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）以及数值模拟，分析系统的分岔结构、稳定性与混沌转变。

Result: 研究表明，即使是最小的无序量也能消除传统logistic映射中的倍周期分岔级联；取而代之的是向高维混沌的转变，该转变由一种此前未在无序系统中报道过的振荡不稳定性标记。此外，当映射之间存在足够强的均匀耦合时，可以恢复传统倍周期分岔级联的部分特征。

Conclusion: 众所周知且经典的动力学系统现象在无序面前可能是脆弱的；同时，无序耦合会催生出既不存在于简单低维动力学中、也不存在于高维无序系统中的全新现象。这提示无序可以深刻重塑动力系统的定性行为。

Abstract: The logistic map is a quintessential model in the study of low-dimensional chaos. High-dimensional chaos, on the other hand, presents itself in disordered systems with many interacting and heterogeneously coupled components. Here, we formulate a system of many logistic maps, interacting through disordered couplings. Using a combination of dynamic mean-field theory, random matrix theory and numerical simulations, we show that even the smallest amount of disorder can remove the period-doubling cascade in the conventional logistic map. Instead we find a transition to high-dimensional chaos, marked by an oscillatory instability not previously reported for disordered systems. We also show that with sufficiently strong homogeneous coupling between the maps one recovers elements of the conventional period-doubling cascade. Our findings indicate that well-known phenomena in dynamical systems can be fragile in the face of disorder. At the same time, new phenomena emerge that are neither found in simple low-dimensional dynamics nor in high-dimensional disordered systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>
